## Best date plans

### 2026-09-15 — NYFF64 ticket on-sale alert + Elder

**NYFF64 ticket on-sale alert, then a meal.** British Indian chophouse at Elder. It is 1.7 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=165+W+65th+St&destination=20+Hudson+Yards%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/) · [Restaurant](https://www.eldernyc.com/)

### 2026-09-11 — Claro + Soft Robotics: An Intro Talk for Curious Artists/Tinkerers

**Dinner, then Soft Robotics: An Intro Talk for Curious Artists/Tinkerers.** Oaxacan Mexican at Claro. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. Soft Robotics: An Intro Talk for Curious Artists/Tinkerers starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=284+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nycresistor.com/2026/09/01/sep-11-2026-soft-robotics-an-intro-talk-for-curious-artists-tinkerers/) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/soft-robotics-an-intro-talk-for-curious-artiststinkerers-tickets-1999421190604) · [Restaurant](https://www.clarobk.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.clarobk.com/reservations)

**Dinner, then Angine de Poitrine with Annie-Claude Deschenes.** Wine bar / vegetable-forward small plates at Ruffian. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. Angine de Poitrine with Annie-Claude Deschenes starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

#### [MoMA free Friday evening](https://www.moma.org/visit/)

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 47.3  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.

## Where to eat

Verified time-sensitive offers and recent restaurant claims against captured source evidence plus limited public follow-up checks; keep items with sourced corrections and reject only duplicate, closed, or unsupported cards.

Dining research: 2026-09-09T10:40:25+00:00

No Michelin-star claims are retained. Source text mentions Michelin Bib/nods for some restaurants, but no Michelin Guide source was checked here; do not surface those as awards or recognition unless separately verified from Michelin's own listing.

### Very British Midweek Lunch

Deals & happy hours — Hawksmoor New York

Official restaurant page lists a weekday-style lunch deal with one, two, or three courses and examples including steak frites, fish and chips, potted beef and bacon, and coronation salad.

Price: 1 course $25; 2 courses $40; 3 courses $55  
Schedule: Monday-Saturday, 12pm-3pm  
Conditions: Available during Hawksmoor's lunch window; the $25/$40/$55 lunch-special pricing is supported by the official location page, while the captured dedicated lunch-menu page showed a sample a la carte/Restaurant Week lunch page rather than the full set-menu terms. Check the restaurant link for the current course list before ordering. Sources: https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/ and https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/food/lunch-menu/  
[Source](https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/food/lunch-menu/)

### Monday Wine Club BYO corkage deal

Deals & happy hours — Hawksmoor New York

Official restaurant page lists a BYO wine option on Mondays, useful for a higher-end steakhouse meal with reduced wine cost.

Price: $10 corkage per bottle  
Schedule: Every Monday, all day  
Conditions: Official location page says Monday, all day: $10 corkage per bottle, up to one bottle per guest. Other food minimums, tax, tip, and service-charge details were not stated. Source: https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/  
[Source](https://thehawksmoor.com/us/locations/new-york/)

### Docks Oyster Bar happy hour at the bar

Deals & happy hours — Docks Oyster Bar

Official event page says Docks runs its oyster happy hour all week at the bar, with oysters, seafood bites, sushi rolls, cocktails, wine, beer, and seafood platters.

Price: PDF menu lists oyster du jour, littleneck clams, and cherrystone clams at $1.50; sushi rolls $11-$24; cocktails mostly $15-$18; Tiny Espresso Tini $14; nonalcoholic Peach & Basil Mule $9; draft beers $6-$8; Yacht Party martini with fries $29.  
Schedule: Daily 3pm-7pm at the bar  
Conditions: Daily 3pm-7pm at the bar. PDF extraction is readable but column order should be checked before assigning any additional item prices. Sources: https://www.docksoysterbar.com/event/happy-hour/ and https://images.getbento.com/accounts/450c5e4dfb658241535f5aca54cf31b4/media/Bx2HqIoCRk2oEsmBjzbb_Docks%20HH%20Menu%20V1%20Summer%202026-5.pdf  
[Source](https://www.docksoysterbar.com/event/happy-hour/)

### Grand Street Summer Restaurant Week 2026

Deals & happy hours — Grand Street BID participating restaurants

Neighborhood promotion, not citywide NYC Restaurant Week. Grand Street BID says 22 East Williamsburg restaurants are offering prix-fixe menus; The Skint listed it as running through 9/10.

Price: Lunch menus $15-$20; dinner menus $22-$40  
Schedule: Through 2026-09-10; specific restaurant days/hours vary and were not fully itemized in fetched text  
Conditions: Grand Street BID says the promotion runs Thursday, August 27 through Thursday, September 10, with prix-fixe menus only during Restaurant Week; ask for the restaurant week menu when seated. Tax and tip not included. Source: https://www.grandstreetbk.org/blog/srw-2026  
From: 2026-08-27  
Through: 2026-09-10  
Published: 2026-08-21  
[Source](https://www.grandstreetbk.org/blog/srw-2026)

### Zara Austin in Forest Hills

Price: Signature cocktails shown at $17; food add-ons include cheese $3, chicken/lamb gyro or chicken shish $6, shrimp $12

### Manhatta bar menu with skyline views

More restaurants — Manhatta

Official menu/location pages make Manhatta useful even without committing to a full dining-room meal: bar snacks and mains include olives, hand-cut chips, oysters, skewers, lobster roll, burger, and fried chicken sandwich.

Schedule: Bar opens daily 11:30am; lunch 11:30am-2pm daily; dinner 5pm-9:30pm Sun-Wed and 5pm-10:30pm Thu-Sat
